using System;
using System.Numerics;
using SharpMediaInfo.Tracks;
using SharpMediaInfo.Native;
using SharpMediaInfo.Native.Enum;
using SharpMediaInfo.Types;

namespace SharpMediaInfo
{
  partial class MediaInfoBuilder
  {
    [global::System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverage]
    private ImageTrack PopulateImageTrack(IMediaInfoNative mediaInfoNative, int index)
    {
      return new ImageTrack
      {
        StreamCount = GetLongValueNullable(mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "StreamCount")),
        StreamKind = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "StreamKind"),
        StreamKindString = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "StreamKind/String"),
        StreamKindID = GetLongValueNullable(mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "StreamKindID")),
        StreamKindPos = GetLongValueNullable(mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "StreamKindPos")),
        StreamOrder = GetLongValueNullable(mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "StreamOrder")),
        FirstPacketOrder = GetLongValueNullable(mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "FirstPacketOrder")),
        ID = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "ID"),
        IDString = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "ID/String"),
        OriginalSourceMediumID = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "OriginalSourceMedium_ID"),
        OriginalSourceMediumIDString = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "OriginalSourceMedium_ID/String"),
        UniqueID = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "UniqueID"),
        UniqueIDString = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "UniqueID/String"),
        MenuID = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "MenuID"),
        MenuIDString = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "MenuID/String"),
        Type = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Type"),
        TypeString = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Type/String"),
        Title = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Title"),
        Format = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Format"),
        FormatString = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Format/String"),
        FormatInfo = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Format/Info"),
        FormatUrl = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Format/Url"),
        FormatCommercial = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Format_Commercial"),
        FormatCommercialIfAny = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Format_Commercial_IfAny"),
        FormatVersion = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Format_Version"),
        FormatProfile = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Format_Profile"),
        FormatCompression = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Format_Compression"),
        FormatAdditionalFeatures = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "Format_AdditionalFeatures"),
        HDRFormat = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "HDR_Format"),
        HDRFormatString = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "HDR_Format/String"),
        HDRFormatCommercial = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "HDR_Format_Commercial"),
        HDRFormatVersion = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "HDR_Format_Version"),
        HDRFormatProfile = mediaInfoNative.GetParameter(StreamKind.Image,index, "HDR_Format_Profile"),
